1182	The eldest brother was called Beran-Setaou. He was[ENTER]responsible for endings and dissolution. Whenever a[ENTER]life cycle drew to its close,[ENTER]he separated body and spirit from each other and guided the[ENTER]soul to the beyond.[ENTER]Thus a constant coming and going - the[ENTER]foundation of all life - was ensured.
